## 2026-09-23 — RI3350 source-semantic correction and offline reanalysis (supersedes v2 disposition)

- **Correction to current state:** The earlier v2 entry below accurately records the evidence available at that time, but its unresolved root-cause and “no reapplication” status was superseded after version-matched SUMO 1.26.0 source evidence became available. It remains preserved as chronology; `PROJECT_STATE.md` now points to the current v3 state.
- **Source/root cause:** The v1_26_0 code path uses integer `SUMOTime` repetition offset `(repetitionEnd - depart) / repetitionNumber`, accumulates it in `incrementFlow()`, defines departure delay as actual minus parameter.depart, and serializes with `time2string()`. The existing run explicitly configures precision 2. For M begin=0, end=1500 s, number=1396, offset=1074 ms; M_flow.16 is scheduled at 17.184 s, departs at 18.000 s, and has exact delay 0.816 s serialized as 0.82 s. Root cause: `ROOT_CAUSE_ADAPTER_ASSUMPTION`.
- **Repair and one offline application:** R04 now reconstructs integer-ms flow times; fixtures include non-divisible intervals and first/middle/last flow indices. `py_compile` and 30/30 tests passed. The adapter was applied once to the existing immutable raw into a new v3 processed directory; no SUMO/netconvert/TraCI process was started. The raw, demand, config, classifier and method were not modified.
- **Independent review:** Data integrity/provenance PASS 0/0/0 High; fixed PRE/control screens fail 30/30 and 150/150 rows. Lane0/lane1 failures and row reasons are preserved. Current locked P/S/L outputs are `NO_QUALIFYING_EVENT`; C is early-warning-only (14 rows); A has no spatial candidate. Control status is `CONTROL_NOT_EVALUABLE`; this does not establish sustained physical congestion or self-deterioration. Historical impact audit finds the patch confined to the RI3350 R04 adapter; no A/B/C, A0 or LOC_M3350 reprocessing is required and old results were not overwritten.
- **Artifacts:** `data/processed/stage6_ramp_induced_validation_20260922_v1/RI3350_CTRL_S17_technical_retry1_postrun_review_v3/` contains SUMO source-semantic notes, R04 application, independent data/screen/historical audits, and consolidated post-run report. The final scientific reviewer requested current-state synchronization after identifying the stale snapshot; it was corrected, and the subsequent final disposition is recorded in the adjacent scientific post-run closeout entry above.
- **Boundary:** No R-on transition was prepared or run. Scientific-input and formal-protocol files are unchanged. Stage 6 remains PARTIAL and O2 NOT_RESOLVED.
- **Subagent routing:** `simulation_engineer` used for source verification, adapter repair and fixtures (PASS); `data_analyst` used for independent raw reconciliation, fixed-screen and historical-impact audits (integrity PASS; screens FAIL as observed); `scientific_reviewer` used for final independent review (in progress after snapshot synchronization).

## Entry Format

## YYYY-MM-DD — Task title

- **Goal:** What this task intended to achieve.
- **Work performed:** What was actually done.
- **Files changed:** Files created or modified.
- **Commands and verification:** Commands, tests, or checks performed.
- **Outcome:** Success, partial success, or failure.
- **Problems and limitations:** Errors, anomalies, or unverified items.
- **Next step:** Safest immediate follow-up.

## 2026-08-23 — SUMO ALINEA technical smoke-test environment

- **Goal:** Establish an isolated SUMO, TraCI, sumolib, and `sumoITScontrol` environment and verify the fixed official ALINEA example as a technical smoke test rather than a formal thesis experiment.
- **Work performed:** Completed project Context Preflight and a read-only environment audit; verified the existing SUMO 1.26.0 macOS Framework installation; created the ignored project `.venv`; installed pinned direct dependencies; acquired only the fixed upstream ALINEA demo inputs from `sumoITScontrol` tag `v0.1.0`; preserved upstream files with SHA-256 provenance; implemented a project wrapper that uses session-level `SUMO_HOME`, temporary runtime copies and detector outputs, GUI/headless selection, explicit seeds, control-call evidence, and SUMO-generated GUI screenshots; and documented the reproducible setup and current technical status.
- **Files changed:** `.gitattributes`, `requirements.txt`, `README.md`, `docs/PROJECT_STATE.md`, `docs/WORKLOG.md`, `src/smoke_tests/run_alinea.py`, `config/smoke_tests/alinea/UPSTREAM.md`, and the fixed upstream files under `config/smoke_tests/alinea/upstream/`. The ignored `.venv/` was created locally. `.gitattributes` preserves the byte-identical upstream whitespace while retaining normal whitespace checks elsewhere.
- **Commands and verification:** Verified macOS 15.3 on Apple M3/arm64, Homebrew 6.0.12, SUMO and `sumo-gui` 1.26.0, and Python 3.13.0; installed `sumoITScontrol==0.1.0`, `traci==1.26.0`, and `sumolib==1.26.0`; `pip check`, package imports, AST/compile checks, wrapper help, static validation, upstream checksum comparison, and mock screenshot timing tests passed. After XQuartz 2.8.6 became available, a GUI run exited 0, completed 8,400 steps, called ALINEA 8,400 times, recorded 69 updates, and generated a 600 s screenshot with 47 vehicles and `J0` state `G`. Headless seed 2 and seed 3 runs both exited 0, completed 8,400 steps and 69 updates, and took approximately 5.63 s and 4.36 s respectively. Runtime outputs were written only under `/private/tmp`.
- **Outcome:** Success for the technical smoke-test scope. The fixed official ALINEA example ran through both GUI and headless execution paths, and the controller was demonstrably invoked. These runs are not formal experiments or thesis evidence.
- **Problems and limitations:** The first GUI attempt failed with an X display error before XQuartz was updated and ready; the successful rerun is the accepted evidence. The upstream demo emits missing-yellow-phase, short-`tau`, collision-teleport, emergency-braking, and deprecated-API warnings. The wrapper records summary metadata and detector outputs but does not persist the full controller time series or stdout/stderr. Python 3.13 satisfies upstream metadata but is not explicitly covered by upstream CI. An unrelated untracked `dashboard/` directory appeared during the task and was not modified, staged, or included.
- **Next step:** Review the upstream warnings and isolate reusable controller-integration patterns before creating any project-owned synthetic scenario; wait for Robert Hilbrich's scope confirmation before formalizing parameters or freezing the experiment protocol.

## 2026-08-31 — Provisional framework, dashboard, and minimal uncontrolled scenario

- **Goal:** Record the user-approved provisional research framework, synchronize the project dashboard, and design and technically validate the smallest project-owned SUMO scenario that can represent freeway traffic, ramp-bound traffic, urban through-traffic, finite ramp storage, and queue spillback.

Both profiles explicitly report `capacity_analysis_eligible=false` and are not thesis evidence.

The formal sweet-spot definition, research-question wording, scope, metrics, thresholds, demand ranges, seeds, and protocol remain unresolved and require later confirmation or authorization.
- **Next step:** Add technical observation points upstream and downstream of the merge, then perform only a small, clearly labelled exploratory uncontrolled `qMain × qRamp` engineering check with per-point realized-insertion gates; complete a reliable visual inspection, prepare a compact evidence package, and ask Robert Hilbrich to confirm the sweet-spot concept and formal scope before any protocol is frozen or formal experiments begin.

## 2026-09-21 — O2 C/S17 U35 targeted causal diagnosis closed

- **Authorization and scope:** Continued the user-authorized technical troubleshooting and bounded C/S17 0–450 s diagnostic. The qMain/qRamp, seed, geometry, signals, restrictions, car-following, lane-changing and other scientific inputs were unchanged. Three SUMO starts were used only to obtain one successful diagnostic; no new demand-grid, replicate batch, controller comparison, formal run, or GUI replay occurred. `data/raw/` remains immutable.
- **Technical failures and exact fixes:** Attempt01 stopped before any traffic step after the observer's 8 s TraCI-listener deadline. The immediate failure was an insufficient readiness window relative to SUMO startup latency; the deeper source of that latency was not established. The readiness deadline was extended to 60 s, with no extra startup attempt. Attempt02 established one TCP/TraCI connection but failed because the observer incorrectly required the SUMO child to retain a `LISTEN` socket after accepting its one allowed client. In SUMO 1.26 the listening socket closes after accept while the established client socket remains valid. The exact fix retained the pre-connect PID-owned-listener check and changed the post-connect check to require the same child PID on the exact established socket tuple. An isolated accepted-socket control verified this lifecycle. Attempt03 then connected and completed all 450 steps. Details and controls are preserved in `artifacts/stage6_o2_causal_diagnostic_20260921_v1/engineering_attempt02_revision02/diagnostics/`, `engineering_attempt03/diagnostics/ATTEMPT02_ROOT_CAUSE_AND_ATTEMPT03_FIX.md`, and the attempt receipts.
- **Successful diagnostic:** Attempt03 card SHA-256 `fa90dd0757acbe7f63b86cd95b27baabedf6977f56c9f9885f9f5593a6ea255c`; exact interval 0–450 s, 450 steps, one SUMO start, rc=0, 7.653689 s, output manifest SHA-256 `1449fe39c02f2cbf1c8faf0d95c535c4e060da83c97e06e36fab627f868a9582`. The attempt produced 49 snapshots. Cumulative technical attempts: 3 starts, 36.196085626012064 s, 14,440,339 bytes. The independent prefix audit passed: all common output roles matched archived C/S17 over the common interval at registered precision; the new leader sampling did not alter any compared existing output. This run is a prefix-neutral diagnostic, not a new replicate.
- **2700 s terminal spatial cross-check:** The 48 unfinished R and 6 unfinished U from archived C/S17 were rechecked against the existing raw FCD/tripinfo with 54/54 identities and positions matching the preserved table. The positions are last observed at label 2699, not exact at termination 2700. Distribution: R=28 ramp_storage, 11 `:urban_diverge_1_0`, 9 shared_approach; U=5 shared_approach, 1 urban_out. The per-vehicle table is `results/tables/stage6_targeted_validation_20260920_v1/revision03/C_terminal_positions.csv` (SHA-256 `51d840fa79c4ce13092335b280f7c6d364d37f40c65c7a5af7b94a7113efa833`), also reproduced in `docs/STAGE6_TARGETED_CAUSAL_VALIDATION_REPORT.md` appendix. These are censored unfinished identities, not arrivals.
- **Mechanism finding:** At U35 labels 395–411, SUMO native foe-inclusive FCD identifies ramp-origin R70 on `:urban_diverge_1_0` as the link leader for U35 on `:urban_diverge_0_0`. R70 is stopped first (397), then U35 (398), R71 (400), R72 (401), and U36 (403); at label 403 the observed chain is R69 ← R70 ← U35 ← R71 ← R72 ← U36. U35 remains partly across the internal/shared boundary through label 412 and clears by 413. This supports a local internal/shared-link obstruction involving R70 and queue propagation, with **Moderate** interpretation confidence. It does not establish R70 as the unique/strongest braking constraint, a faulty priority rule, the full meter-to-U causal chain, or a geometry defect. Native FCD and TraCI `getLeader` differ by SUMO API semantics: the latter filters this different-outgoing-edge foe; its `None` does not rebut the native relation. The reported 2.50 m link/crossing effective gap is not a measured Euclidean or bumper gap.
- **Storage/detector interpretation:** The registered strict `storage_cross=0` and 317.57 m cumulative path definition remain unchanged. The path comprises 113.08 m internal connector plus 204.49 m ramp storage; E2 covers the storage segment and shared approach, not the internal connector, while FCD observes the internal segment. Therefore E2 coverage mismatch does not explain the U35 event, but the strict criterion still does not establish continuous meter-anchored stopped spillback over the full path. Existing evidence supports ramp-origin queue presence together with a local internal/shared obstruction; it does not cleanly classify the case as pure full-storage spillback or as an independent junction fault. No measurement or geometry edit was made.
- **Final independent scientific review:** `PASS_FINAL_SCIENTIFIC_REVIEW`; Blocker/Major/required Minor `0/0/0`. The only minor from revision03 was closed in the TLS sentence. Bound report revision04 SHA-256 `1996b2b9852b0345f72f38bbaed97d535c9ef3a50cf23905af1df19ce2852c41`, manifest SHA-256 `72368e1ab30493cc96831740b66a017a485de2f8ff29e6c948a6073baccaa7b7`, receipt SHA-256 `13d88b87b4b683986649c8cbed62c9b3370d35cef3528b5a0f55a12ab0ce9cec`; all 19 bindings match. ## 2026-09-21 — A/B/C freeway-pressure and measurement-scope diagnosis

- **Question:** Used existing matched A/B/C seed17 raw outputs to distinguish weak baseline freeway pressure from insufficient detector/aggregation scope. No SUMO/TraCI/netconvert/GUI process or new scientific run was started; no parameter, threshold, geometry, or protocol was changed.
- **Observed A event:** M775 slowed from 28.16 m/s at t=918 on `main_up` to 10.97 m/s at t=924 on `merge_section_1` position 110.36 m, then recovered to 27.06 m/s at t=933. This rules out “no freeway disturbance at all,” but the single-seed record does not establish a sustained or protection-worthy breakdown. No M sample in A/B/C met the existing `<0.1 m/s` stop rule; that does not exclude moving congestion. No approved moving-breakdown threshold or duration rule exists, so none was added.
- **Measurement finding:** The `merge20` E1 point is upstream of the observed minimum; 30 s bins smooth brief individual events, and the old 300 s start window omits early B/C minima. The complete 0–2700 s E1 grid is intact, and 1 Hz M-FCD/internal-edge tracks plus local M travel-time bounds cover and capture the merge-local event. Thus limited E1 placement/aggregation is real, but a total measurement-blindspot explanation is not supported. Local TT bounds cross zero; M-only unique downstream passages remain 1071 in each condition. Mixed M/R E1 passage counts were not used as M protection evidence.
- **Interpretation:** `INSUFFICIENT_MAINLINE_PRESSURE` remains unidentified (mechanism confidence Low–Moderate); the stronger claim that all observed freeway degradation was outside current data coverage is not supported (coverage audit confidence High). Lack of clearly demonstrated sustained baseline pressure is more consistent with the absent consistent protection pattern, but meter-program suitability and stochastic realization remain alternatives. This diagnosis does not resolve O2, `specific_obstacle`, or Stage 6.
- **Derived package and review:** 101 analyst checks passed; independent scientific review passed for exploratory use with Blocker/Major/required Minor 0/0/0. Report SHA-256 `9190237d76126578bce6bde6f2473b531f923beb9688a05f7c6a8d09edb5a92c`; manifest and receipt are in `data/processed/stage6_o2_freeway_pressure_diagnosis_20260921_v1/`. No raw output was altered.

## 2026-09-26 — A-to-B mainline metric clarification for Robert draft

- In response to the speed-only concern, independently checked existing default-model A/B22/B28 raw-bound summaries and scientific reviews. Fixed active M core speed is A26.964/B22 26.572/B28 25.376 m/s; full M1396 mean trip 73.564/73.830/74.285 s; M-only first downstream passage by1500 is 1345 in all three. B22 differences are small; B28's local speed reduction is larger; neither supports M protection. Equal cumulative passage does not establish equal time-varying flow or capacity, and all-M trip means include pre-R vehicles. No new simulation or formal inference.
- Question: whether one local mean speed is sufficient to judge freeway impact/protection. FHWA's MOE typology distinguishes speed, travel time, delay, utilization/throughput, queues and density (`https://www.fhwa.dot.gov/publications/research/operations/11064/004.cfm`); its ramp-management guidance weighs freeway improvement against ramp delay/queue (`https://ops.fhwa.dot.gov/publications/ramp_mgmt_handbook/manual/manual/5_1.htm`). Neither gives a transferable numerical threshold for this synthetic merge. Methodological judgment: report fixed local M speed together with full M trip, M-only passage and spatial/temporal accumulation, with each metric's scope and causal caveat explicit. This clarification is reflected in unsent `artifacts/stage6_exploratory_closeout_20260926_v1/ROBERT_EMAIL_CN_DRAFT_V2.md`; V1 is retained. No decision or protocol status changed.
